# Event responses (historical observation, measured from the lake)

Generated by `python -m india.eventresp report`. Returns against the close before the first tradable session. Sectors: return minus Nifty 50. This describes what happened; it is not evidence that the event caused it (confounders are listed per event). 2024+ events are not measured here (holdout).
